DEAR SHAREHOLDERS,                                                        各位股東：
On behalf of the board of directors (the “Board”) of Nissin Foods         本人謹此代表日清食品有限公司（「本公司」）董事
Company Limited (the “Company”), I am very pleased to present the         會（「董事會」）欣然呈報本公司連同其附屬公司
Annual Report of the Company and its subsidiaries (the “Group”) for       （
                                                                          「本集團」）截至2020年12月31日止年度之年報。
the year ended 31 December 2020.

FINANCIAL PERFORMANCE                                                     財務表現
In the midst of the COVID-19 pandemic, thanks to all the Group’s staff    在COVID-19疫情期間，有賴本集團全體員工共同
who have been working hard together to ensure a stable supply of          努力，以確保產品供應穩定並抱持集團創始人安
products and sharing the belief of the Group’s founder, Mr. Momofuku      藤百福先生所提倡的「食為聖職」信念，本集團於
Ando, that “Food-related job is a sacred profession (『食為聖職』)”,            回顧年度內取得穩定表現。本集團的本公司擁有
the Group achieved a resilient performance for the year under review.     人應佔溢利較上一個財政年度增加約20.3%，並
The Group’s profit attributable to owners of the Company grew by          連續第二年錄得高盈利按年增長。本集團的收入
approximately 20.3% over that of last financial year and it achieved a    同比增長14.0%，主要由於社交距離措施及新的
high profit growth year-on-year for the second consecutive year. The      正常生活促使需求激增，尤其中國業務收入連續
Group’s revenue grew by 14.0% year-on-year mainly due to the surge        第三年達到人民幣雙位數增長。
in demand prompted by social distancing measures and a new normal
life. In particular, the revenue for the PRC operation achieved double-
digit growth in the RMB term for the third consecutive year.

Despite all production lines were temporarily suspended in the PRC in     儘管所有在中國的生產線在2020年2月曾短暫停
February 2020 in compliance with the relevant requirement imposed         工，以遵守當地政府為遏止COVID-19擴散而實施
by the local governments to curb the spread of COVID-19, the Group        的相關規定，本集團仍不斷透過擴展地理業務區
has continuously delivered unique foods, including Cup Noodles (合味        域及渠道提供獨特的食品品牌，包括合味道及出
道) brand and Demae Iccho (出前一丁) brand through the expanded                前一丁。袋裝麵條（如出前一丁）的需求因香港及
geographic business territories and channels. Particularly, the demand    中國的「宅經濟」尤其增加。此外，非麵類產品（包
for bag-typed noodles such as Demae Iccho (出前一丁) increased due to         括冷凍食品、穀物麥片及Kagome蔬果汁）的銷量
stay-at-home economy in both Hong Kong and the PRC. Furthermore,          增加亦為整體財務業績帶來增長。
the increase in sales in non-noodles category including frozen foods,
granola, potato chips and Kagome vegetable and fruit juice contributed
to overall financial performance.

ambiguous. Not until the vaccinations become available to most of the      苗後，經濟才能重新起動，世界才能回復平靜。

Risk Management and Internal Control                                            風險管理及內部監控
The Group has formulated and adopted the risk management policy in              本集團已制定及採納風險管理政策以為識別、評
providing direction in identifying, evaluating and managing significant         估及管理主要風險提供指導。主要風險管理程序
risks. The main features of the risk management processes comprise 5            包括五個核心流程：(a) 風險確定；(b) 風險評估及
core stages: (a) risk identification, (b) risk assessment and prioritisation,   其風險優先次序；(c) 風險應對；(d) 風險監控；及
(c) risk response, (d) risk monitoring and (e) risk reporting.                  (e) 風險匯報。


The appropriate risk mitigation plans are determined based on the               本集團根據以下風險應對措施釐定適當的風險減
following risk responses:                                                       緩計劃：


–     Acceptance: Risks are considered immaterial and acceptable                －   接受：根據本集團之風險接受程度，風險被
      based on Group’s risk appetite and therefore no action is                     視為並不重大及可接受，故毋須採取任何行
      considered necessary.                                                         動。


–     Reduction: Risks cannot be considered immaterial, actions such as         －   降低：風險被視為不能忽視，須採取監控措
      controls have to be taken to reduce the impact and vulnerability              施等行動以減少影響及風險承受程度至可接
      to an acceptable level.                                                       受水平。


–     Sharing: Risks cannot be considered immaterial and the Group              －   分擔：風險被視為不能忽視，本集團本身無
      itself cannot effectively reduce the risks to an acceptably low               法自行將風險有效降至可接受的水平，故須
      level, therefore a portion of the risks has to be transferred to or           透過保險及外判等方式將部分風險轉移至或
      shared with other parties by insurance, outsourcing, etc.                     與其他各方共同分擔。


–     Avoidance: Risks are so significant that there is no measure              －   防範：風險被視為相當重大，本集團沒有足
      both internally and externally to reduce the risk to an acceptable            夠及有效的內部監管系統及外界措施可將風
      level, or involve unreasonably high cost to reduce the risk to                險降至可接受水平或需不合理及高昂之成本
      an acceptable level. Therefore, activities giving rise to the risks           將風險降至可接受水平。因此，須避開產生
      should be avoided.                                                            有關風險之活動。


The Group has adopted the COSO ERM Framework, the “Top-Down”                    本集團已採納 COSO 的風險管理框架（
                                                                                                   「由上而下」
approach, in establishing its risk management framework, which                  方式）由董事會、審核委員會及風險管理專責小組
consists of the Board, the Audit Committee, the Risk Management                 及各有關部門組成的風險管理及內部監控架構。
Taskforce and various departments within the Group.


The Board acknowledges that it is responsible for the risk management           董事會深明其負責風險管理及內部監控系統，並
and internal control systems and reviewing their effectiveness. The             檢討其成效。董事會釐定達致本集團業務目標所
Board determines the nature and extent of risks that shall be taken             涉及之風險性質及程度，並為監控風險管理及內
in achieving the Group’s business objectives, and has the overall               部監控系統之設計、實施及整體有效性整體負責。

effectiveness of risk management and internal control systems.

     restrictions on the payment of dividends; and (iii) any other factors that
     the Board considers relevant.

PERMITTED INDEMNITY PROVISION                                                獲准許之彌償條文
Pursuant to the Articles of Association, every directors shall be entitled   根據組織章程細則並在公司條例准許之情況下，
to the indemnity out of the assets of the Company against any liability      每名董事均有權就其涉及本公司之任何民事或刑
incurred by him/her in relation to the Company in defending any              事訴訟進行抗辯而招致之任何負債，從本公司資
proceedings, whether civil or criminal, to the extent permitted by the       產中獲得彌償。有關獲准許之彌償條文已於截至
Companies Ordinance. Such permitted indemnity provision was in force         2020 年 12 月 31 日止年度生效，並於本年報日期
during the year ended 31 December 2020 and remains in force at the           一直生效。
date of this annual report.


EMOLUMENT POLICY                                                             薪酬政策
The emolument policy of the Directors of the Company is decided              本公司董事之薪酬政策由薪酬委員會考慮到本公
by the Remuneration Committee, having regard to the Company’s                司之表現、經營業績、可比較市場統計數據而決
performance, operating results and comparable market statistics.             定。

the year and up to the date of this annual report, the Company has           持上市規則規定之足夠公眾持股量。

     independence, and where applicable, actions taken to eliminate threats   脅或應用防範措施。
     or safeguards applied.


     From the matters communicated with those charged with governance,        從與治理層溝通的事項中，我們確定哪些事項對
     we determine those matters that were of most significance in the audit   本期綜合財務報表的審計最為重要，因而構成
     of the consolidated financial statements of the current period and       關鍵審計事項。我們在核數師報告中描述這些事
     are therefore the key audit matter. We describe these matters in our     項，除非法律法規不允許公開披露這些事項，或
     auditor’s report unless law or regulation precludes public disclosure    在極端罕見的情況下，如果合理預期在我們報告
     about the matter or when, in extremely rare circumstances, we            中溝通某事項造成的負面後果超過產生的公眾利
     determine that a matter should not be communicated in our report         益，我們決定不應在報告中溝通該事項。
     because the adverse consequences of doing so would reasonably
     be expected to outweigh the public interest benefits of such
     communication.

     Impacts on application of Amendments to HKAS 1                            應用香港會計準則第1號及香港會計準
     and HKAS 8 Definition of Material                                         則第8號（修訂本）重要性的定義的影響
     The Group has applied the Amendments to HKAS 1 and HKAS                   本集團於本年度首次應用香港會計準則第
     8 for the first time in the current year. The amendments provide          1號及香港會計準則第8號（修訂本）。該修
     a new definition of material that states “information is material         訂本為重要性一詞提供新定義，說明「倘遺
     if omitting, misstating or obscuring it could reasonably be               漏、錯誤陳述或隱瞞資訊可以合理預期會影
     expected to influence decisions that the primary users of general         響一般用途財務報表的主要使用者基於該等
     purpose financial statements make on the basis of those financial         提供有關特定報告實體之財務資訊的財務報
     statements, which provide financial information about a specific          表作出之決策，則該資訊屬重要」。該修訂
     reporting entity.” The amendments also clarify that materiality           本亦釐清在整體財務報表的範圍內，重要性
     depends on the nature or magnitude of information, either                 取決於資訊的性質或幅度（單獨或與其他資
     individually or in combination with other information, in the             訊結合使用）。
     context of the financial statements taken as a whole.


     The application of the amendments in the current year had no              於本年度應用有關修訂對綜合財務報表並無
     impact on the consolidated financial statements.                          影響。


     Impacts on application of Amendments to HKFRS 3                           應用香港財務報告準則第3號（修訂本）
     Definition of a Business                                                  業務的定義的影響
     The Group has applied the amendments for the first time in the            本集團於本年度首次應用有關修訂。該等修
     current year. The amendments clarify that while businesses usually        訂本澄清，儘管業務通常具有產出，但一組
     have outputs, outputs are not required for an integrated set of           經整合的活動及資產毋須產出亦符合資格構
     activities and assets to qualify as a business. To be considered a        成業務。所收購的一組活動及資產至少須包
     business, an acquired set of activities and assets must include,          括一項投入及一項實質過程並共同顯著促進
     at a minimum, an input and a substantive process that together            創造產出的能力，方會被視作業務。
     significantly contribute to the ability to create outputs.

     2.   APPLICATION OF AMENDMENTS TO HONG KONG FINANCIAL                        2.   應用香港財務報告準則（「香
          REPORTING STANDARDS (“HKFRSs”) (continued)                                   港財務報告準則」）的修訂
                                                                                       （續）
          Amendments to HKFRSs that are mandatorily                                    於本年度強制生效之經修訂香港財務報
          effective for the current year (continued)                                     （續）
                                                                                       告準則
          Impacts on application of Amendments to HKFRS 3                              應用香港財務報告準則第3號（修訂本）
          Definition of a Business (continued)                                         業務的定義的影響（續）
          The amendments remove the assessment of whether market                       該等修訂本刪除市場參與者是否有能力取代
          participants are capable of replacing any missing inputs or                  任何缺失的投入或過程及持續製造產出的評
          processes and continuing to produce outputs. The amendments                  估。該等修訂本亦引入額外指引，有助釐定
          also introduce additional guidance that helps to determine                   是否已獲得實質過程。
          whether a substantive process has been acquired.


          In addition, the amendments introduce an optional concentration              此外，該等修訂本引入一項可選集中性測
          test that permits a simplified assessment of whether an acquired             試，允許對所收購的一組活動及資產是否為
          set of activities and assets is not a business. Under the optional           一項業務進行簡化評估。根據可選集中性測
          concentration test, the acquired set of activities and assets is not         試，倘所收購的總資產的絕大部分公平值均
          a business if substantially all of the fair value of the gross assets        集中於單個可識別資產或一組類似資產中，
          acquired is concentrated in a single identifiable asset or group             則所收購的一組活動及資產並非一項業務。
          of similar assets. The gross assets under assessment exclude cash            經評估的總資產不包括現金及現金等價物、
          and cash equivalents, deferred tax assets, and goodwill resulting            遞延稅項資產及遞延稅項負債的影響所產生
          from the effects of deferred tax liabilities. The election on                的商譽。可按個別交易基準選擇是否應用該
          whether to apply the optional concentration test is available on             可選集中性測試。
          transaction-by-transaction basis.


          The application of the amendments had no impact on the                       應用有關修訂對本年度的綜合財務報表並無
          consolidated financial statements in the current year as similar             影響，因為若不採用可選集中性測試亦會達
          conclusion would have been reached without applying the                      致相若結論。
          optional concentration test.

          Amendment to HKFRS 16 Covid-19-Related Rent                                香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第 1 6 號（修 訂 本）
          Concessions                                                                2019冠狀病毒相關租金寬減
          The amendment is effective for annual reporting periods                    有關修訂於2020年6月1日或之後開始的年
          beginning on or after 1 June 2020.                                         度報告期間生效。


          The amendment introduces a new practical expedient for lessees             該修訂本為承租人引進了新的可行權宜方法
          to elect not to assess whether a Covid-19-related rent concession          使其可選擇不評估Covid-19相關租金減免是
          is a lease modification. The practical expedient only applies              否為一項租賃修訂。該可行權宜方法僅適用
          to rent concessions occurring as a direct consequence of the               於滿足以下所有條件由Covid-19直接產生的
          Covid-19 that meets all of the following conditions:                       租金減免：


          •     the change in lease payments results in revised                      •   租賃付款變動導致的租賃的經修訂代
                consideration for the lease that is substantially the same               價與緊接變動前的租賃代價基本相同
                as, or less than, the consideration for the lease immediately            或低於該代價；
                preceding the change;


          •     any reduction in lease payments affects only payments                •   租賃付款的減少僅影響原定於2021年
                originally due on or before 30 June 2021; and                            6月30日或之前到期的付款；及


          •     there is no substantive change to other terms and                    •   租賃的其他條款及條件並無實質性變
                conditions of the lease.                                                 動。


          A lessee applying the practical expedient accounts for changes             應用可行權宜方法將租金減免導致的租賃付
          in lease payments resulting from rent concessions the same way             款變動列賬的承租人將以同一方式將應用香
          it would account for the changes applying HKFRS 16 Leases if               港財務報告準則第16號「租賃」的變動入賬
          the changes are not a lease modification. Forgiveness or waiver            （倘變動並非租賃修訂）。租賃付款的寬免或
          of lease payments are accounted for as variable lease payments.            豁免入賬列作可變租賃付款。相關租賃負債
          The related lease liabilities are adjusted to reflect the amounts          獲調整以反映寬免或豁免的金額，並於該事
          forgiven or waived with a corresponding adjustment recognised              項發生的期間內在損益中確認相應調整。
          in the profit or loss in the period in which the event occurs.


          The application is not expected to have impact on the Group’s              由於本集團不擬應用可行權宜方法，故預計
          financial position and performance as the Group does not intend            應用有關修訂將不會對本集團的財務狀況及
          to apply the practical expedient.                                          表現造成影響。

          3.1 Basis of preparation of consolidated financial                           3.1 綜合財務報表編製基準
              statements
                The consolidated financial statements have been prepared                 綜合財務報表乃根據香港會計師公會
                in accordance with the HKFRSs issued by the HKICPA. For                  所頒佈之香港財務報告準則編製。
                the purpose of preparation of the consolidated financial                 就編製綜合財務資料而言，倘有關資
                statements, information is considered material if such                   料合理預期會影響主要用戶作出的決
                information is reasonably expected to influence decisions                定，則有關資料被視為重大。此外，
                made by primary users. In addition, the consolidated                     綜合財務報表亦包括香港聯合交易所

                by the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on The                  例規定之適用披露資料。
                Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited and by the Hong
                Kong Companies Ordinance.


                The consolidated financial statements have been prepared                 除下文會計政策所詳述若干於各報告
                on the historical cost basis except for certain financial                期末按公平值計量之金融工具外，綜
                instruments that are measured at fair values at the end                  合財務報表乃按歷史成本基準編製。
                of each reporting period, as explained in the accounting
                policies set out below.


                Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the              歷史成本通常基於交換貨品及服務時
                consideration given in exchange for goods and services.                  所提供代價之公平值。

                Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an                公平值為於計量日期市場參與者於有
                asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction          秩序交易中出售資產可收取或轉讓負
                between market participants at the measurement date,                     債須支付之價格，而不論該價格是否
                regardless of whether that price is directly observable or               直接可觀察或可使用其他估值技術估
                estimated using another valuation technique. In estimating               計。倘市場參與者於計量日期對資產
                the fair value of an asset or a liability, the Group takes into          或負債定價時會考慮資產或負債之特
                account the characteristics of the asset or liability if market          點，則本集團於估計資產或負債之公
                participants would take those characteristics into account               平值時會考慮該等特點。該等綜合財
                when pricing the asset or liability at the measurement date.             務報表中作計量及╱或披露用途之公
                Fair value for measurement and/or disclosure purposes in                 平值乃按此基準釐定，惟屬於香港財
                these consolidated financial statements is determined on                 務報告準則第2號以股份為基礎付款
                such a basis, except for share-based payment transactions                範圍內之以股份為基礎付款交易、根
                that are within the scope of HKFRS 2 Share-based                         據香港財務報告準則第16號入賬的租
                Payment , leasing transactions that are accounted for in                 賃交易，以及與公平值有部分相若地
                accordance with HKFRS 16, and measurements that have                     方但並非公平值之計量，譬如香港會
                some similarities to fair value but are not fair value, such as          計準則第2號存貨 內之可變現淨值或
                net realisable value in HKAS 2 Inventories or value in use in            香港會計準則第36號資產減值 之使用

                 The consolidated financial statements incorporate the                  綜合財務報表包括本公司以及由本公

                 when the Company:                                                      制權：


                 •     has power over the investee;                                     •   對投資對象之權力；

                       involvement with the investee; and                                   承擔風險或擁有權利；及

                                                                                            報。


                 The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee            倘有事實及情況顯示上述三項控制權

                 to one or more of the three elements of control listed                 會重新評估其是否對投資對象擁有控
                 above.                                                                 制權。


                 Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Company                  本公司獲得附屬公司控制權時將附屬
                 obtains control over the subsidiary and ceases when the                公司綜合入賬，並於失去附屬公司控
                 Company loses control of the subsidiary. Specifically,                 制權時終止綜合入賬。具體而言，於
                 income and expenses of a subsidiary acquired or disposed               年內收購或出售附屬公司之收入及開
                 of during the year are included in the consolidated                    支，按自本公司獲得控制權當日起至

                 income from the date the Company gains control until the               入綜合損益及其他全面收入表內。
                 date when the Company ceases to control the subsidiary.


                 Profit or loss and each item of other comprehensive income             損益及其他全面收入之各項目乃歸屬
                 are attributed to the owners of the Company and to the                 於本公司擁有人及非控制性權益。附
                 non-controlling interests. Total comprehensive income of               屬公司之全面收入額總歸屬於本公司
                 subsidiaries is attributed to the owners of the Company                擁有人及非控制性權益，即使此舉會

                 the non-controlling interests having a deficit balance.

          whether an acquired set of activities and assets is not a                    及資產是否構成一項業務進行簡化評
          business. The concentration test is met if substantially all of              估。倘所收購的總資產的絕大部分公

          is needed.                                                                   估。


          Asset acquisitions                                                           資產收購
          When the Group acquires a group of assets and liabilities                    當本集團收購一組資產及負債不構成
          that do not constitute a business, the Group identifies and                  一項業務時，本集團首先透過按金
          recognises the individual identifiable assets acquired and                   融資產╱金融負債各自的公平值分配
          liabilities assumed by allocating the purchase price first                   購買價，以識別及確認取得的個別可
          to financial assets/financial liabilities at the respective fair             識別資產及承擔的負債，餘下購買價
          values, the remaining balance of the purchase price is then                  結餘其後按各自於購買日期的相對公
          allocated to the other identifiable assets and liabilities on                平值分配至其他可識別資產及負債。
          the basis of their relative fair values at the date of purchase.             該等交易不會產生商譽或議價購買收
          Such a transaction does not give rise to goodwill or bargain                 益。
          purchase gain.

                 allocated to each of the Group’s cash-generating units (or            會從合併獲得協同效益之本集團各

                 from the synergies of the combination, which represent the            別），即商譽就內部管理而言之最低
                 lowest level at which the goodwill is monitored for internal          監控水平，且不會大於營運分部。
                 management purposes and not larger than an operating
                 segment.

                 which goodwill has been allocated is tested for impairment            告期末之前進行減值測試。倘現金產
                 before the end of that reporting period. If the recoverable           生單位之可收回金額低於其賬面值，
                 amount is less than its carrying amount, the impairment               則減值虧損會首先分配，以降低單位
                 loss is allocated first to reduce the carrying amount of any          獲分配之任何商譽賬面值，其後按單
                 goodwill and then to the other assets on a pro-rata basis             位（或現金產生單位組別）內各項資產
                 based on the carrying amount of each asset in the unit (or            賬面值按比例分配至其他資產。
                 group of cash-generating units).

          Investment in an associate                                                 於聯營公司的投資
          An associate is an entity over which the Group has                         聯營公司為本集團對其擁有重大影響
          significant influence. Significant influence is the power to               力的實體。重大影響力為可參與投資
          participate in the financial and operating policy decisions                對象之財政及營運政策決策而非對該

          policies.


          The results and assets and liabilities of associate are                    聯營公司的業績與資產及負債使用權
          incorporated in these consolidated financial statements                    益會計法綜合計入該等綜合財務報
          using the equity method of accounting. The financial                       表。就權益會計法而言，所用聯營公
          statements of associate used for equity accounting                         司財務報表採用與本集團在相若情況
          purposes are prepared using uniform accounting policies                    下就類似交易及事件採用之會計政策
          as those of the Group for like transactions and events                     一致者編製。根據權益法，於聯營公
          in similar circumstances. Under the equity method, an                      司之投資初步按成本於綜合財務狀況
          investment in an associate is initially recognised in the                  表內確認，其後之調整則確認本集團
          consolidated statement of financial position at cost and                   分佔聯營公司之損益及其他全面收
          adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s share of the                  入。聯營公司資產淨值之變動（損益
          profit or loss and other comprehensive income of the                       及其他全面收入除外）不予入賬，除
          associate. Changes in net assets of the associate other than               非該等變動導致本集團於聯營公司所
          profit or loss and other comprehensive income are not                      持擁有權權益出現變動。當本集團分
          accounted for unless such changes resulted in changes in                   佔聯營公司之虧損超出其於該聯營公
          ownership interest held by the Group. When the Group’s                     司之權益，則本集團不再繼續確認其

          in that associate, the Group discontinues recognising its                  僅以本集團已產生法定或推定責任或

          only to the extent that the Group has incurred legal or

                 Over time revenue recognition: measurement                             按時間確認收益：計量完全達成
                 of progress towards complete satisfaction of a                         履約責任的進度
                 performance obligation
                 Output method                                                          輸出法
                 The progress towards complete satisfaction of a                        完全達成履約責任的進度按輸出法計
                 performance obligation is measured based on output                     量，該輸出法根據直接計量至今已轉
                 method, which is to recognise revenue on the basis of                  移予客戶的貨品或服務價值與該合約
                 direct measurements of the value of the goods or services              項下承諾的餘下貨品或服務相比較確
                 transferred to customers to date relative to the remaining             認收益，可以最佳方式描述本集團轉
                 goods or services promised under the contract, that best               移貨品或服務控制權的履約情況。
                 depict the Group’s performance in transferring control of
                 goods or services.


                 As a practical expedient, if the Group has a right to                  作為一項可行權宜方法，倘本集團有
                 consideration in an amount that corresponds directly                   權收取與本集團至今已完成履約價值
                 with the value of the Group’s performance completed to                 （即本集團就所提供服務每月固定金
                 date (i.e. service contracts in which the Group bills a fixed          額的服務合約）直接對應的金額，則
                 amount for each month of service provided), the Group                  本集團有權按本集團有權發票的金額
                 recognises revenue in an amount to which the Group has                 確認收益。
                 the right to invoice.


                 Variable consideration                                                 可變代價
                 For contracts that contain variable consideration, such                就訂有可變代價（如銷售回贈）之合約
                 as sales rebate, the Group estimates the amount of                     而言，本集團以(a)預期價值法；或(b)
                 consideration to which it will be entitled using either (a)            最有可能獲得之金額估計其可獲得之
                 the expected value method or (b) the most likely amount,               代價金額，取於那方法較佳預測本集
                 depending on which method better predicts the amount of                團可獲得之代價金額。
                 consideration to which the Group will be entitled.


                 The estimated amount of variable consideration is                      可變代價之估計金額乃包含於交易價
                 included in the transaction price only to the extent that              格內，惟僅當可變代價之相關不確
                 it is highly probable that such an inclusion will not result           定性在其後變得確定，致使有關估計
                 in a significant revenue reversal in the future when the               金額很大可能不會導致於將來出現重
                 uncertainty associated with the variable consideration is              大收益撥回，其方可包含於交易價格
                 subsequently resolved.                                                 內。

      3.   BASIS OF PREPARATION OF CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL                        3.   綜合財務報表編製基準及主
           STATEMENTS AND SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)                 要會計政策（續）
           3.2 Significant accounting policies (continued)                                     （續）
                                                                                      3.2 主要會計政策
                 Leases                                                                 租賃
                 Definition of a lease                                                  租賃的定義
                 A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys            倘合約為換取代價而給予在一段時間
                 the right to control the use of an identified asset for a              內控制可識別資產使用的權利，則該
                 period of time in exchange for consideration.                          合約是租賃或包含租賃。


                 For contracts entered into or modified on or after the date            就於首次應用日期或之後或因業務合
                 of initial application or arising from business combinations,          併而訂立或修改的合約而言，本集團
                 the Group assesses whether a contract is or contains a lease           根據香港財務報告準則第16號的定義
                 based on the definition under HKFRS 16 at inception or                 於初始或修改日期（如適用）評估該合
                 modification date, as appropriate. Such contract will not be           約是否為租賃或包含租賃。除非合約
                 reassessed unless the terms and conditions of the contract             的條款及條件其後出現變動，否則有
                 are subsequently changed. As a practical expedient, leases             關合約將不予重新評估。作為簡易實
                 with similar characteristics are accounted on a portfolio              務處理方法，當本集團合理預期對綜
                 basis when the Group reasonably expects that the effects               合財務報表的影響將不會與組合內的
                 on the consolidated financial statements would not differ              個別租賃顯著不同時，具有類似特徵
                 materially from individual leases within the portfolio.                的租賃按組合基準入賬。


                 The Group as a lessee                                                  本集團作為承租人
                 Allocation of consideration to components of a contract                將代價分配至合約的組成部分
                 For a contract that contains a lease component and one or              就包含租賃組成部分以及一項或多項
                 more additional lease or non-lease components, the Group               額外租賃或非租賃組成部分的合約而
                 allocates the consideration in the contract to each lease              言，本集團依照租賃組成部分的相對
                 component on the basis of the relative stand-alone price of            獨立價格及非租賃組成部分的總獨立
                 the lease component and the aggregate stand-alone price                價格，將合約中的代價分配至各組賃
                 of the non-lease components.                                           組成部分。


                 Non-lease components are separated from lease                          非租賃部份獨立於租賃部份，並應用
                 component and are accounted for by applying other                      其他適用準則處理。
                 applicable standards.


                 Short-term leases                                                      短期租賃
                 The Group applies the short-term lease recognition                     本集團對自開始日期起計租期為12個
                 exemption to leases of staff quarters that have a lease term           月或以下且不包含購買選擇權的員工
                 of 12 months or less from the commencement date and do                 宿舍租賃應用短期租賃的確認豁免。
                 not contain a purchase option. Lease payments on short-                短期租賃的租賃付款在租賃期內採用
                 term leases are recognised as expense on a straight-line               直線基準或其他系統性基準確認為支
                 basis or another systematic basis over the lease term.                 出。

          Right-of-use assets                                                      使用權資產
          The cost of right-of-use asset includes:                                 使用權資產的成本包括：


          •     the amount of the initial measurement of the lease                 •        租賃負債的初步計量金額；
                liability;


          •     any lease payments made at or before the                           •        於開始日期或之前作出的任何
                commencement date, less any lease incentives                                租賃付款，減任何已收租賃優
                received;                                                                   惠；

                                                                                            及

                dismantling and removing the underlying assets,                             產、復原相關資產所在場地或

                terms and conditions of the lease.                                          本估計。

          adjusted for any remeasurement of lease liabilities.                     何重新計量作出調整。


          Right-of-use assets in which Group is reasonably certain to              本集團於租期屆滿時合理確定獲得相
          obtain ownership of the underlying leased assets at the end              關租賃資產的所有權的使用權資產
          of the lease term are depreciated from commencement                      於開始日期至使用期結束內折舊。否
          date to the end of the useful life. Otherwise, right-of-use              則，使用權資產於其估計使用期及租
          assets are depreciated on a straight-line basis over the                 期（以較短者為準）內按直線法基準折
          shorter of its estimated useful life and the lease term.                 舊。


          The Group presents right-of-use assets as a separate line                本集團於綜合財務狀況表內將使用權
          item on the consolidated statement of financial position.                資產呈列為個別項目。

          Taxation                                                                  稅項
          Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently                所得稅開支指即期應付稅項及遞延稅
          payable and deferred tax.                                                 項之總和。


          The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit                      即期應付稅項按年內應課稅溢利計
          for the year. Taxable profit differs from profit before                   算。應課稅溢利與「除稅前溢利」不
          taxation because of income or expense that are taxable                    同，乃由於其他年度之應課稅或可扣
          or deductible in other years and items that are never                     稅收入或開支以及從毋須課稅或不能
          taxable or deductible. The Group’s liability for current tax              扣稅之項目。本集團之即期稅項負債
          is calculated using tax rates that have been enacted or                   乃根據於報告期末已頒佈或實質上已
          substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.                 頒佈之稅率計算。

          between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the             負債賬面值與計算應課稅溢利所用
          consolidated financial statements and the corresponding                   相應稅基之暫時性差額確認。遞延稅
          tax bases used in the computation of taxable profit.                      項負債一般就所有應課稅暫時性差額
          Deferred tax liabilities are generally recognised for                     確認。遞延稅項資產一般會於可能出
          all taxable temporary differences. Deferred tax assets                    現應課稅溢利以抵銷此等可扣稅之暫
          are generally recognised for all deductible temporary                     時性差額時就所有可扣稅之暫時性差
          differences to the extent that it is probable that taxable                額確認。倘暫時性差額源自初步確認
          profits will be available against which those deductible                 （除業務合併外）不影響應課稅溢利或
          temporary differences can be utilised. Such deferred tax                  會計溢利之交易之資產及負債，則有
          assets and liabilities are not recognised if the temporary                關遞延稅項資產及負債不會予以確
          difference arises from the initial recognition (other than                認。此外，倘於初步確認商譽後產生
          in a business combination) of assets and liabilities in a                 暫時性差額，遞延稅項負債將不會予
          transaction that affects neither the taxable profit nor the               以遞延。
          accounting profit. In addition, deferred tax liabilities are
          not recognised if the temporary difference arises from the
          initial recognition of goodwill.


          Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary             遞延稅項負債乃就於附屬公司及聯營
          differences associated with investments in subsidiaries and               公司投資相關之應課稅暫時性差額
          an associate, except where the Group is able to control the               確認，惟本集團可控制有關暫時性差
          reversal of the temporary difference and it is probable that              額撥回且於可見將來可能不會撥回除
          the temporary difference will not reverse in the foreseeable              外。僅於有足夠應課稅溢利可用於抵
          future. Deferred tax assets arising from deductible                       銷暫時性差額之抵免且預期暫時性差
          temporary differences associated with such investments are                額於可見將來撥回時，方會確認該等

          the benefits of the temporary differences and they are
          expected to reverse in the foreseeable future.

          over their estimated useful life. The estimated useful life                  年期及攤銷方法於各報告期末進行檢
          and amortisation method are reviewed at the end of each                      討，而任何估計變動之影響按預期基
          reporting period, with the effect of any changes in estimate                 準入賬。
          being accounted for on a prospective basis.


          Research and development expenditure                                         研發開支
          Expenditure on research activities is recognised as an                       研究活動之開支在產生期間確認為開
          expense in the period in which it is incurred.                               支。


          Intangible assets acquired in a business combination                         業務合併中所收購無形資產
          Intangible assets acquired in a business combination                         業務合併中收購的無形資產與商譽分
          are recognised separately from goodwill and are initially                    開確認及初步按其於收購日期的公平
          recognised at their fair value at the acquisition date (which is             值（視作其成本）確認。
          regarded as their cost).


          Subsequent to initial recognition, intangible assets                         於初步確認後，於業務合併中所收購
          acquired in a business combination with finite useful life                   可使用年期為有限的無形資產按成
          are reported at costs less accumulated amortisation and                      本減累計攤銷及任何累計減值虧損呈
          any accumulated impairment losses, on the same basis as                      報，其基準與單獨收購的無形資產相
          intangible assets that are acquired separately.                              同。


          An intangible asset is derecognised on disposal, or when                     無形資產於出售時或預期不能再透過
          no future economic benefits are expected from use or                         使用或出售取得未來經濟利益時終止
          disposal. Gains and losses arising from derecognition of                     確認。終止確認無形資產所產生之收
          an intangible asset, measured as the difference between                      益或虧損按出售所得款項淨額與有關
          the net disposal proceeds and the carrying amount of the                     資產賬面值之差額計量，並於終止確
          asset, are recognised in profit or loss when the asset is                    認該資產之期間於損益表中確認。
          derecognised.

                 Impairment on property, plant and equipment,                           物業、廠房及設備、使用權資產

                 At the end of the reporting period, the Group reviews the              本集團於報告期末審閱其具有限可使

                 life to determine whether there is any indication that                 等資產是否出現減值虧損跡象。如有

                 indication exists, the recoverable amount of the relevant              可收回金額以釐定減值虧損（如有）之

                 impairment loss, if any.

                 recoverable amount of the cash-generating unit to which                收回金額。
                 the asset belongs.


                 In testing a cash-generating unit for impairment, corporate            於測試現金產生單位之減值時，當合

                 when a reasonable and consistent basis of allocation can be            資產分配予相關現金產生單位，否則

                 group of cash generating units for which a reasonable                  準的最小現金產生單位組合內。可收
                 and consistent allocation basis can be established. The                回金額是按公司資產所屬的現金產生
                 recoverable amount is determined for the cash-generating               單位或現金產生單位組合釐定，並與
                 unit or group of cash-generating units to which the                    相關現金產生單位或現金產生單位組
                 corporate asset belongs, and is compared with the carrying             合的賬面值進行比較。
                 amount of the relevant cash-generating unit or group of
                 cash-generating units.


                 Recoverable amount is the higher of fair value less costs              可收回金額為公平值減出售成本與使
                 of disposal and value in use. In assessing value in use, the           用價值之較高者。評估使用價值時，
                 estimated future cash flows are discounted to their present            估計未來現金流量採用稅前折現率折
                 value using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current              現至其現值，該折現率反映當前市場
                 market assessments of the time value of money and the                  所評估之貨幣時間價值及資產（或現
                 risks specific to the asset (or a cash-generating unit) for            金產生單位）之特定風險，而估計未
                 which the estimates of future cash flows have not been                 來現金流量尚未作出調整。
                 adjusted.

           3.2 Significant accounting policies (continued)                                       （續）
                                                                                        3.2 主要會計政策
                 Inventories                                                              存貨
                 Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net                      存貨按成本與可變現淨值兩者之較低
                 realisable value. Costs of inventories are determined on a               者列賬。存貨成本按「先入先出」方法
                 first-in, first-out method. Net realisable value represents              釐定。可變現淨值指存貨之估計售價
                 the estimated selling price for inventories less all estimated           減全部估計完成成本及進行出售之必
                 costs of completion and costs necessary to make the sale.                要成本。


                 Provisions                                                               撥備
                 Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present                   撥備於本集團由於過往事件而產生現
                 obligation (legal or constructive) as a result of a past event,          有的法定或推定責任，本集團可能須
                 it is probable that the Group will be required to settle that            解決有關責任，以及能夠可靠地估計
                 obligation, and a reliable estimate can be made of the                   解決有關責任的金額時確認。
                 amount of the obligation.


                 The amount recognised as a provision is the best estimate                確認為撥備之金額乃於報告期末對解
                 of the consideration required to settle the present                      決當前責任所需代價的最佳估計，並
                 obligation at the end of the reporting period, taking                    計及有關責任所涉及的風險及不確定
                 into account the risks and uncertainties surrounding the                 因素。倘撥備乃按估計用以償付當前
                 obligation. When a provision is measured using the cash                  責任所需現金流量計算，其賬面值為
                 flows estimated to settle the present obligation, its carrying           該等現金流量之現值（倘金錢時間價
                 amount is the present value of those cash flows (where the               值的影響為重大）。
                 effect of the time value of money is material).


                 Financial instruments                                                    金融工具
                 Financial assets and financial liabilities are recognised                當集團實體成為工具合約條文之一方
                 when a group entity becomes a party to the contractual                   時，會確認金融資產及金融負債。所
                 provisions of the instrument. All regular way purchases or               有以正規途徑購買或出售之金融資產
                 sales of financial assets are recognised and derecognised                乃按交易日期基準確認及終止確認。
                 on a trade date basis. Regular way purchases or sales are                以正規途徑購買或出售為要求在法規
                 purchases or sales of financial assets that require delivery             或慣例所確定之時間框架內於市場交
                 of assets within the time frame established by regulation or             付資產之金融資產購買或出售。
                 convention in the marketplace.

          Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from                        全期預期信貸虧損指相關工具於預計
          all possible default events over the expected life of the                    全期內所有可能違約事件所產生的預
          relevant instrument. In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m                         期信貸虧損。相反，12個月預期信貸
          ECL”) represents the portion of lifetime ECL that is                         虧損（
                                                                                         「12個月預期信貸虧損」
                                                                                                    ）指預計
          expected to result from default events that are possible                     於報告日期後12個月內可能發生的
          within 12 months after the reporting date. Assessments are                   違約事件產生的部分全期預期信貸虧
          done based on the Group’s historical credit loss experience,                 損。評估乃根據本集團過往信貸虧損
          adjusted for factors that are specific to the debtors, general               經驗，並就債務人特定因素、一般經
          economic conditions and an assessment of both the current                    濟狀況以及對於報告日期之當時狀況
          conditions at the reporting date as well as the forecast of                  及未來狀況預測的評估作調整。
          future conditions.


          The Group always recognises lifetime ECL for trade                           本集團一直確認貿易應收賬款及應收
          receivables and amounts due from fellow subsidiaries                         同系附屬公司款項（屬貿易性質）之全
          which are in trade nature. The ECL on these assets are                       期預期信貸虧損。該等資產的預期信
          assessed individually for debtors with significant balances                  貸虧損乃就具有重大結餘的債務人進
          and collectively using a provision matrix with appropriate                   行個別評估及使用合適分組的撥備矩
          groupings. The amounts due from subsidiaries which are in                    陣進行整體評估。應收附屬公司款項
          trade nature are assessed individually.                                     （屬貿易性質）乃個別評估。


          For all other instruments, the Group measures the loss                       就所有其他工具而言，本集團計量虧
          allowance equal to 12m ECL, unless when there has been                       損撥備等於12個月預期信貸虧損，
          a significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition,             除非當信貸風險自初始確認以來顯著
          in which case the Group recognises lifetime ECL. The                         上升，則本集團確認全期預期信貸虧
          assessment of whether lifetime ECL should be recognised is                   損。評估是否應確認全期預期信貸虧
          based on significant increases in the likelihood or risk of a                損乃根據自初始確認以來發生違約的
          default occurring since initial recognition.                                 可能性或風險顯著上升。

                 (i)   Significant increase in credit risk                              (i)   信貸風險大幅增加
                       In assessing whether the credit risk has increased                     於評估信貸風險是否自初始確
                       significantly since initial recognition, the Group                     認以來大幅增加時，本集團比
                       compares the risk of a default occurring on the                        較金融工具於報告日期出現違
                       financial instrument as at the reporting date with the                 約的風險與該金融工具於初始
                       risk of a default occurring on the financial instrument                確認日期出現違約的風險。作
                       as at the date of initial recognition. In making this                  此評估時，本集團會考慮合理
                       assessment, the Group considers both quantitative                      及有理據的定量及定性資料，
                       and qualitative information that is reasonable and                     包括歷史經驗及毋須花費不必
                       supportable, including historical experience and                       要成本或精力即可獲得的前瞻
                       forward-looking information that is available without                  性資料。
                       undue cost or effort.


                       In particular, the following information is taken into                 尤其是，評估信貸風險是否大
                       account when assessing whether credit risk has                         幅增加時會考慮下列資料：
                       increased significantly:


                       •     an actual or expected significant deterioration                  •   金融工具外部（如有）或內
                             in the financial instrument’s external (if                           部信貸評級的實際或預期
                             available) or internal credit rating;                                重大惡化；


                       •     significant deterioration in external market                     •   信貸風險的外界市場指標
                             indicators of credit risk, e.g. a significant                        的重大惡化，例如信貸息
                             increase in the credit spread, the credit default                    差大幅增加、債務人的信
                             swap prices for the debtor;                                          貸違約掉期價；


                       •     existing or forecast adverse changes in                          •   預期將導致債務人履行其
                             business, financial or economic conditions                           債務責任的能力大幅下降
                             that are expected to cause a significant                             的業務、財務或經濟狀況
                             decrease in the debtor’s ability to meet its debt                    的現有或預測不利變動；
                             obligations;


                       •     an actual or expected significant deterioration                  •   債務人經營業績的實際或
                             in the operating results of the debtor;                              預期重大惡化；


                       •     an actual or expected significant adverse                        •   導致債務人履行其債務責
                             change in the regulatory, economic, or                               任的能力大幅下降的債務
                             technological environment of the debtor that                         人監管、經濟或技術環境
                             results in a significant decrease in the debtor’s                    的實際或預期重大不利變
                             ability to meet its debt obligations.                                動。

                when it has an internal or external credit rating                              外部信用評級為「投資級別」，

                 (v)   Measurement and recognition of ECL                               (v)   預期信貸虧損的計量及確認
                       The measurement of ECL is a function of the                            預期信貸虧損的計量為違約概
                       probability of default, loss given default (i.e. the                   率 、 違 約 損 失 率（即 存 在違 約
                       magnitude of the loss if there is a default) and                       時 的 損 失 程 度）及 違 約 風險 的
                       the exposure at default. The assessment of the                         函數。違約概率及違約損失率
                       probability of default and loss given default is                       乃基於根據前瞻性資料和歷史
                       based on historical data and forward-looking                           數據評估。預期信貸虧損估計
                       information. Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased                    反映公正及機率加權金額，此
                       and probability-weighted amount that is determined                     乃按相關違約發生風險為加權
                       with the respective risks of default occurring as the                  而釐定。本集團採用撥備矩陣
                       weights. The Group uses a practical expedient in                       估計貿易應收款項的預期信貸
                       estimating ECL on trade receivables using a provision                  虧損，並考慮過往信貸虧損經
                       matrix taking into consideration historical credit loss                驗，並就不過多投入資金或精
                       experience, adjusted for forward looking information                   力即可獲得的前瞻性資料而作
                       that is available without undue cost or effort.                        調整。


                       Generally, the ECL is the difference between all                       一般而言，預期信貸虧損為本
                       contractual cash flows that are due to the Group                       集團根據合約應收的所有合約
                       in accordance with the contract and the cash flows                     現金流量與本集團預計收取的
                       that the Group expects to receive, discounted at                       現金流量之間的差額，並按初
                       the effective interest rate determined at initial                      始確認時釐定的實際利率貼
                       recognition.                                                           現。


                       Lifetime ECL for certain trade receivables are                         若干貿易應收款項的全期預期
                       considered on a collective basis taking into                           信貸虧損按逾期資料及相關信
                       consideration past due information and relevant                        貸資料（如前瞻宏觀經濟資料）
                       credit information such as forward looking                             等綜合基準考量。
                       macroeconomic information.

                 Financial instruments (continued)                                         金融工具（續）

                 Derecognition of financial assets                                         終止確認金融資產
                 The Group derecognises a financial asset only when the                    僅當資產現金流量之合約權利屆滿
                 contractual rights to the cash flows from the asset expire,               時，或其將金融資產以及資產擁有權
                 or when it transfers the financial asset and substantially all            之絕大部分風險及回報轉移予另一實
                 the risks and rewards of ownership of the asset to another                體時，本集團方會終止確認該金融資
                 entity.                                                                   產。


                 On derecognition of a financial asset measured at                         於終止確認按攤銷成本計量之金融資
                 amortised cost, the difference between the asset’s carrying               產時，資產賬面值與已收及應收代價
                 amount and the sum of the consideration received and                      間之差額於損益內確認。
                 receivable is recognised in profit or loss.


                 Financial liabilities and equity                                          金融負債及權益
                 Classification as debt or equity                                          分類為債務或權益
                 Debt and equity instruments are classified as either financial            債務及權益工具乃根據合約安排之內
                 liabilities or as equity in accordance with the substance                 容與金融負債及權益工具之定義分類
                 of the contractual arrangements and the definitions of a                  為金融負債或權益。
                 financial liability and an equity instrument.


                 Equity instruments                                                        權益工具
                 An equity instrument is any contract that evidences a                     權益工具為證明經扣除其所有負債後
                 residual interest in the assets of an entity after deducting all          之實體資產剩餘權益之任何合約。由
                 of its liabilities. Equity instruments issued by the Company              本公司發行之權益工具乃按所收取所
                 are recognised at the proceeds received, net of direct issue              得款項（扣除直接發行成本）確認。
                 costs.


                 Financial liabilities at amortised cost                                   按攤銷成本列賬之金融負債
                 Financial liabilities (including trade payables, other payables           金融負債（包括貿易應付款項、其他
                 and amounts due to ultimate holding company and fellow                    應付款項及應計費用及應付最終控股
                 subsidiaries) are subsequently measured at amortised cost,                公司及同系附屬公司款項）其後將使
                 using the effective interest method.                                      用實際利息法按攤銷成本計量。

     In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which are              於應用本集團之會計政策時（詳述於附註
     described in Note 3, the directors of the Company are required to             3)，本公司董事須對未能透過其他來源確定
     make judgements, estimates and assumptions about the carrying                 之資產及負債賬面值作出判斷、估計及假
     amounts of assets and liabilities that are not readily apparent               設。所作出之估計及相關假設乃以過往經驗
     from other sources. The estimates and underlying assumptions                  及其他被視為相關之因素為基準。實際業績
     are based on historical experience and other factors that are                 可能與該等估計有異。
     considered to be relevant. Actual results may differ from these
     estimates.


     The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on an                   估計及相關假計乃按持續基準審閱。倘對會
     ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised               計估計之修訂僅影響進行修訂之期間，則於
     in the period in which the estimate is revised if the revision affects        該期間確認，或倘修訂會影響目前及未來期
     only that period, or in the period of the revision and future                 間，則會於修訂及未來期間確認。
     periods if the revision affects both current and future periods.

           Control over the charitable fund                                             對慈善基金的控制
           During the year ended 31 December 2020, a charitable fund                    截至2020年12月31日止年度，本集團已成
           was established by the Group (the “Fund”), and HK$40,000,000                 立一項慈善基金（「基金」），並向基金捐出
           was donated by the Group to the Fund. The directors and senior               40,000,000港元。本公司董事及高級管理層
           management of the Company were nominated as members of                       獲提名為諮詢委員會成員，負責就所有可能
           the advisory committee which advise and make recommendations                 的慈善捐款向金融機構提供意見及建議。
           to the financial institution on all prospective charitable donations.


           The directors of the Company assessed whether the Group has                  本公司董事根據本集團是否擁有基金相關活
           control over the Fund based on whether the Group has the                     動的可變回報，評估本集團是否擁有基金的
           variable returns from the relevant activities of the Fund. In making         控制權。作出判斷時，本公司董事考慮本集
           the judgement, the directors of the Company considered the                   團是否有能力控制捐款用途及經濟利益可否
           Group’s ability to direct the usage of donations and the possibility         返還予本集團。經評估後，本公司董事認為
           of economic benefit flowing back to the Group. After assessment,             本集團並無具有基金相關活動的可變回報，
           the directors of the Company concluded that the Group does not               因此對基金並無控制權。
           have variable returns from the relevant activities of the Fund and
           therefore the Group does not have control over the Fund.


           Donations of HK$40,000,000 was recognised in profit or loss                  截至2020年12月31日止年度，40,000,000
           during the year ended 31 December 2020.                                      港元捐款已於損益中確認。

      10. INCOME TAX EXPENSE (continued)                                         10. 所得稅開支（續）
           On 21 March 2018, the Hong Kong Legislative Council passed              於2018年3月21日，香港立法會通過2017
           The Inland Revenue (Amendment) (No. 7) Bill 2017 (the “Bill”)           年稅務（修訂）
                                                                                         （第7號）條例草案（「條例草
           which introduced the two-tiered profits tax rates regime. The           案」
                                                                                    ），其引入兩級制利得稅率制度。條例草
           Bill was signed into law on 28 March 2018 and was gazetted on           案於2018年3月28日簽署成為法律並於翌日
           the following day. Under the two-tiered profits tax rates regime,       刊登憲報。根據兩級制利得稅率制度，合資
           the first HK$2,000,000 of profits of the qualifying group entity        格集團實體的首2百萬港元溢利將按8.25%
           will be taxed at 8.25%, and profits above HK$2,000,000 will be          的稅率徵稅，而超過2百萬港元的溢利將按
           taxed at 16.5%. The profits of group entities not qualifying for        16.5%的稅率徵稅。不符合兩級制利得稅率

35. RETIREMENT BENEFITS SCHEME                                          35. 退休福利計劃
    The Group participates in both defined contribution scheme            本集團參與根據職業退休計劃條例註冊之定
    which is registered under the Occupational Retirement Schemes         額供款計劃（「職業退休計劃」）及於2000年
    Ordinance (the “ORSO Scheme”) and a Mandatory Provident               12月根據強制性公積金計劃條例成立之強
    Fund Scheme (the “MPF Scheme”) established under the                  制性公積金計劃（
                                                                                 「強積金計劃」
                                                                                       ）。該等計劃
    Mandatory Provident Fund Schemes Ordinance in December                之資產與本集團資產分開處理，由受託人管
    2000. The assets of the schemes are held separately from those        理之基金持有。在強積金計劃成立前為職業
    of the Group, in funds under the control of trustees. Certain         退休計劃成員之若干僱員仍然參與職業退休
    employees who were members of the ORSO Scheme prior to                計劃，對於在2000年12月1日或之後加入本
    the establishment of the MPF Scheme remained with the ORSO            集團之所有新僱員則必須參與強積金計劃。
    Scheme, whereas all new employees joining the Group on or
    after 1 December 2000 are required to join the MPF Scheme.


    For members of the MPF Scheme, the Group contributes 5%               就強積金計劃成員而言，本集團及其僱員各
    of relevant payroll costs to the Scheme, which contribution is        自須按僱員工資之5%向計劃作出供款，每
    matched by the employee and capped at HK$1,500 per month.             月供款上限為1,500港元。


    The ORSO Scheme is funded by monthly contributions from               職業退休計劃由僱員及本集團按僱員基本薪
    both employees and the Group at 5% of the employees’ basic            金之5%每月作出供款。自損益扣除之退休
    salary. The pension costs charged to profit or loss represent         金成本指本集團按該等計劃規定之比率已作
    contributions paid or payable to these funds by the Group at          出或應作出之供款。
    rates specified in the rules of these schemes.


    The employees of the Company’s subsidiaries in the PRC are            本公司之中國附屬公司僱員參與中國政府管
    members of the state-managed retirement benefits scheme               理之國家管理退休福利計劃。本公司中國附
    operated by the PRC government. The Company’s PRC                     屬公司須按僱員工資之一定百分比向該退休
    subsidiaries are required to contribute a certain percentage of       福利計劃供款，以撥付該項福利所需款項。
    their employees’ payroll costs to the retirement benefits scheme      本集團於該退休福利計劃內之唯一責任為根
    to fund the benefits. The only obligation of the Group with           據該計劃支付指定供款額。
    respect to the retirement benefits scheme is to make the required
    contributions under the scheme.


    The total expense recognised in profit or loss of HK$33,597,000       於損益確認之總開支33,597,000港元（2019
    (2019: HK$58,198,000) represents contributions payable to these       年：58,198,000港元）指本集團按計劃規則
    plans by the Group at rates specified in the rules of the plans.      所指定比例向該等計劃應作出供款。

    plan and which are available to reduce the contributions payable      供款之沒收供款。

37. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                37. 資本風險管理
    The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the             本集團之資本管理旨在確保本集團旗下實體
    Group will be able to continue as a going concern while                  將能持續經營，同時透過經營產生之溢利為
    maximising the return to shareholders through the generation of          利益相關方爭取最高回報。本集團之整體策
    profits from its operations. The Group’s overall strategy remains        略較過往年度維持不變。
    unchanged from prior year.


    The capital structure of the Group consists of cash and cash             本集團資本結構包括現金及現金等價物及本
    equivalents and equity attributable to owners of the Company,            公司擁有人應佔權益（包括已發行股本及儲
    comprising issued share capital and reserves.                            備）。


    The directors of the Company review the capital structure on an          本公司董事按年度基準審閱資本結構。作為
    annual basis. As part of this review, the directors consider the         此審閱之一環，董事考慮資本成本及與資本
    cost of capital and the risks associated with its capital. Based on      相關之風險。本集團將根據董事之意見透過
    recommendations of the directors, the Group will balance its             支付股息及發行新股來平衡其整體資本結
    overall capital structure through the payment of dividends and           構。
    new share issues.
